Human annotators also struggled to identify synthetic credibility, reaching only 63% TPR. These findings establish synthetic credibility as a severe and underexplored visual misinformation challenge, and provide a benchmark for developing detectors that reason beyond superficial credibility cues.

</details>


### [51] [P\textsuperscript{2}-DPO: Grounding Hallucination in Perceptual Processing via Calibration Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03376)
*Ruipeng Zhang,Zhihao Li,Haozhang Yuan,C. L. Philip Chen,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P²-DPO，一种让模型自主生成并学习自身偏好对的新型训练范式，通过聚焦注意力区域的感知增强和视觉鲁棒性，并引入校准损失对齐视觉信号与文本生成，有效解决LVLM中的幻觉问题，在基准测试上超越依赖昂贵人类反馈的强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法未能针对性解决LVLM中注意力区域的感知瓶颈和对图像退化的视觉鲁棒性不足问题，且偏好对通常视觉无关且为离策略性质，限制了模型学习效果。

Method: 提出P²-DPO范式，包含：(1) 针对焦点增强感知和视觉鲁棒性的在策略偏好对构建方法；(2) 校准损失（Calibration Loss）用于精确对齐视觉信号与文本的因果生成。

Result: 在相当训练数据和成本下，P²-DPO在基准测试上超越依赖昂贵人类反馈的强基线；在注意力区域保真度（ARF）和图像退化场景评估中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: P²-DPO通过自生成在策略偏好对和校准损失，有效解决了感知瓶颈和视觉鲁棒性问题，为LVLM幻觉缓解提供了更高效、更精准的训练范式。

Abstract: Hallucination has recently garnered significant research attention in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aims to learn directly from the corrected preferences provided by humans, thereby addressing the hallucination issue. Despite its success, this paradigm has yet to specifically target the perceptual bottleneck in attended regions or address insufficient Visual Robustness against image degradation. Furthermore, existing preference pairs are often vision-agnostic and their inherently off-policy nature limits their effectiveness in guiding model learning. To address these challenges, we propose Perceptual Processing Direct Preference Optimization (P\textsuperscript{2}-DPO), a novel training paradigm in which the model generates and learns from its own preference pairs, thereby directly addressing the identified visual bottlenecks while inherently avoiding the issues of vision-agnostic and off-policy data. It introduces: (1) an on-policy preference pairs construction method targeting Focus-and-Enhance perception and Visual Robustness, and (2) a well-designed Calibration Loss to precisely align visual signals with the causal generation of text. Experimental results demonstrate that with a comparable amount of training data and cost, P\textsuperscript{2}-DPO outperforms strong baselines that rely on costly human feedback on benchmarks. Furthermore, evaluations on Attention Region Fidelity (ARF) and image degradation scenarios validate the effectiveness of P\textsuperscript{2}-DPO in addressing perceptual bottleneck in attended regions and improving Visual Robustness against degraded inputs.

</details>


### [52] [Towards Characterizing Scientific Image Utility and Upgradability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03401)
*WenZhe Li,Qihang Yan,Liang Chen,Junying Wang,Farong Wen,Yijin Guo,Chunyi Li,Zicheng Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIU²A框架，从Utility（错误检测与修复可行性）和Upgradability（修复质量）两个维度评估科学图像，并构建了包含四种腐败类型的基准数据集。实验表明现有多模态系统在科学错误评估与忠实修复方面存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图像作为研究传播中的关键证据，其完整性面临AI生成内容带来的前所未有的威胁，而现有评估范式（感知质量指标、语言模型验证能力）均无法有效评估科学图像的有效性。

Method: 提出SIU²A框架，定义Utility（错误检测+修复可行性）与Upgradability（修复质量）两个互补维度；将科学图像腐败分为Detail Distortion、Incompleteness、False Content、Entity Confusion四类；构建含专家标注的SIU²A-Benchmark数据集；实现两阶段评估协议。

Result: 实验表明当前多模态系统在科学错误评估与忠实修复方面存在显著局限，暴露了视觉感知与科学可用性之间的根本差距。

Conclusion: 现有AI系统无法可靠地评估和修复科学图像中的错误，所提框架为科学图像评估提供了新的维度和基准。

Abstract: Scientific images function as critical evidence in research communication, yet their integrity faces unprecedented threats from AI-generated content that introduces subtle but consequential errors. Existing evaluation paradigms prove inadequate: perceptual quality metrics poorly correlate with scientific validity, while language models lack domain-specific verification capabilities. To address this gap, we propose the \textbf{S}cientific \textbf{I}mage \textbf{U}tility and \textbf{U}pgradability \textbf{A}ssessment (\textbf{SIU$^2$A}) framework, which introduces two complementary dimensions for scientific image evaluation. \textbf{Utility} encompasses \textit{error detection} (identifying scientific inaccuracies) and \textit{correction feasibility} (assessing whether errors can be reliably repaired). \textbf{Upgradability} measures the quality of correction. We categorize scientific image corruption into four fundamental types: Detail Distortion, Incompleteness, False Content, and Entity Confusion. Based on this taxonomy, we construct SIU$^2$A-Benchmark, a dataset with expert annotations for error identification and repair. The framework implements a two-stage evaluation protocol: the \textit{Utility} stage evaluates error detection capability and repair instruction generation, while the \textit{Upgradability} stage assesses whether corrections faithfully restore scientific validity without compromising existing accurate information. Experiments reveal that current multimodal systems exhibit significant limitations in both scientific error assessment and faithful correction, exposing a fundamental gap between visual perception and scientific usability.

</details>


### [53] [Mamba-Enhanced Implicit Motion Learning for Audio-Driven Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03402)
*Xuan Wei,Jiahui Chen,Kaiheng Li,Mingyu Shao,Qingqi Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于隐式运动框架的音频驱动人体运动视频生成方法，采用两阶段流水线解耦运动预测与渲染，结合区域感知注意力机制和Mamba增强扩散模型，在380小时高质量数据集上训练，达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于关键点的方法难以捕捉细微运动动态，需要更有效的隐式运动建模方式来生成真实且时间连贯的人体运动视频。

Method: 两阶段流水线：第一阶段集成外观先验和分层深度线索到区域感知注意力机制中建模隐式运动特征；第二阶段采用Mamba增强扩散模型直接从音频和源图像预测这些特征，实现无监督学习细粒度运动模式。

Result: 在多个公开基准和自建数据集上，在准确度、自然度和时间连贯性方面均超越此前方法，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 解耦的运动预测与渲染架构增强了灵活性和效率，大规模高质量数据集训练进一步提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Audio-driven human motion video generation aims to synthesize realistic and temporally coherent human animations from a single static image, with applications in talking-head synthesis, co-speech gesture generation, and dynamic presentations. Moving beyond conventional keypoint-based methods that often struggle to capture subtle motion dynamics, We propose a novel implicit-motion framework for generating realistic and temporally coherent human motion videos from a single static image and audio. Our approach uses a two-stage pipeline that decouples motion prediction from rendering. The first stage integrates appearance priors and hierarchical depth cues into a region-aware attention mechanism to model latent motion features. The second stage employs a Mamba-enhanced diffusion model to directly predict these features from audio and the source image, enabling unsupervised learning of fine-grained motion patterns. This decoupled architecture enhances flexibility and efficiency. Trained on a new 380-hour high-quality dataset, our method outperforms prior work across multiple public benchmarks and our collected data in accuracy, naturalness, and temporal coherence, setting a new state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [54] [SAMatcher: Co-Visibility Modeling with Segment Anything for Robust Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03406)
*Xu Pan,Qiyuan Ma,Mingyue Dong,He Chen,Wei Ji,Xianwei Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMatcher提出了一种基于共视性建模的特征匹配框架，利用SAM的对称交叉视图交互机制预测共视区域掩码和边界框作为结构化先验，通过统一监督方案联合优化掩码学习、框回归和一致性约束，在视角和尺度变化大的场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的特征匹配方法多在像素或块级别操作，缺乏对多视图间联合可见区域的显式建模，导致在大视角和尺度变化下匹配性能受限。

Method: 基于SAM（Segment Anything Model），提出对称交叉视图交互机制实现双向特征交换和跨视图语义对齐；通过预测共视区域掩码和边界框作为结构化先验；设计统一监督方案联合优化掩码学习、框回归和掩码-框一致性约束。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准上显著优于现有匹配流程，尤其在大视角和尺度变化场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 原本用于单目分割的基础模型可以通过显式的共视性建模有效扩展到多视图对应推理，为图像匹配的结构化表示学习提供了新视角。

Abstract: Reliable correspondence estimation is a fundamental problem in image processing, underpinning applications such as Structure from Motion, visual localization, and image registration. Existing learning-based methods have significantly improved local feature representations, yet most still operate at the pixel or patch level and lack explicit modeling of regions that are jointly visible across views. We propose SAMatcher, a feature matching framework that formulates correspondence estimation through co-visibility modeling. Instead of directly matching local features, SAMatcher first predicts co-visible region masks and bounding boxes as structured priors for correspondence estimation. Built upon the Segment Anything Model (SAM), it introduces a symmetric cross-view interaction mechanism that enables bidirectional feature exchange and cross-view semantic alignment. We further develop a unified supervision scheme that jointly optimizes mask prediction and box localization through mask learning, box regression, and mask-box consistency constraints. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate substantial improvements over existing matching pipelines, particularly under large viewpoint and scale variations. Our results show that foundation models originally designed for monocular segmentation can be effectively extended to multi-view correspondence reasoning through explicit co-visibility modeling, offering a new perspective on structured representation learning for image matching. Code and project page: https://xupan.top/Projects/samatcher

</details>


### [55] [Enginuity: A Dataset and Benchmark for Vision-Language Understanding of Engineering Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03410)
*Abhishek Kumar,Isha Motiyani,Tilak Kasturi,Ethan Seefried,Prahitha Movva,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Enginuity，首个面向工程图纸的视觉语言模型公开基准，涵盖结构化零件表提取和自由形式视觉问答两个任务。实验表明现有VLM在零件描述保真度上存在系统性缺陷，并揭示token重叠指标在技术描述评估中低估模型能力达2-6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程图纸对视觉语言模型构成独特挑战（密集空间布局、领域特定符号、视觉标注与零件表的交叉引用），但缺乏公开基准来评估VLM在此领域的能力，现有数据集主要聚焦流程图、科学图表或商业文档。

Method: 构建基于美军维修手册的Enginuity数据集，定义结构化零件表提取（Task 1）和自由形式视觉问答（Task 2）两个任务，在零样本和思维链提示下评估GPT-5.2 Chat、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemma 4、Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct四个前沿VLM。

Result: Task 1中Recall@all达0.61-0.87但Token F1pen仅0.03-0.18，暴露零件识别与描述保真度之间的系统性差距。Task 2揭示所有模型存在一致的事实推理差距。token重叠指标比语义相似度低估技术描述能力2-6倍。

Conclusion: 现有VLM在工程图纸理解上存在显著不足，需要领域特定评估方法；token重叠指标不适用于技术描述评估，应以LLM-as-judge语义相似度作为校准手段；发布完整数据集和评估工具链以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Engineering diagrams pose a distinct challenge for vision-language models: unlike natural images or general documents, they encode information through dense spatial layouts, domain-specific symbols, and cross-references between visual callouts and structured parts tables. Despite their centrality to service, repair, and design workflows, there is no public benchmark for measuring VLM capabilities in this domain; existing datasets primarily focus on flowcharts, scientific figures, or business documents. To address this gap, we introduce Enginuity, the first open dataset and benchmark for evaluating VLMs on complex engineering diagrams. We define two tasks over a corpus of U.S. military service and repair manuals: structured parts-table extraction (Task 1) and free-form visual diagram question answering (VQA)(Task 2) for benchmarking. We evaluate four frontier VLMs (GPT-5.2 Chat, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemma 4, Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct) under zero-shot and chain-of-thought prompting. On Task 1, models reach Recall@all of 0.61-0.87 but Token F1pen of only 0.03-0.18, exposing a systematic gap between part identification and description fidelity. Task 2 reveals a consistent factual-reasoning gap across all models. A supporting analysis shows that token-overlap metrics under-report model capability on technical descriptions by 2-6x relative to semantic similarity, motivating LLM-as-judge calibration for domain-specific evaluation. We release the dataset, annotations, evaluation harness, and per-sample model outputs to support a reproducible study of VLM capability on engineering content.

</details>


### [56] [PHAF-Personalized Hand Avatars in a Flash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03420)
*Meghana Shankar,Akanxit Upadhyay,Anmol Namdev,Green Rosh KS,Pawan Prasad BH*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PHAF是一种从两张手部图片（手背和手掌视图）快速生成个性化逼真手部化身的方法，相比传统优化方法速度提升30倍，适用于AR/VR实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于优化的手部化身生成方法速度慢，难以在边缘设备上实时部署，无法满足AR/VR应用对快速个性化手部建模的需求。

Method: 结合语义引导的网格对齐和稠密纹理提取技术高效迁移高频细节；采用基于视图的修补网络优化纹理，确保平滑连续的外观；利用参数化手部模型实现精确关节运动。

Result: 在视觉保真度上与现有方法相当，但纹理生成时间缩短了30倍，支持新颖视角泛化和实时部署。

Conclusion: PHAF实现了从两张图片快速生成高质量个性化手部化身，大幅提升效率，为实际AR/VR应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present PHAF-Personalized Hand Avatars in a Flash, a personalized photo-realistic hand avatar which provides high quality multi-view renders from just two images (dorsal and palmar views).Unlike slow optimization-based techniques, PHAF generates fast personalized textures for real-time deployment on edge devices. Our approach combines semantic guided mesh alignment and densified texture extraction to transfer high-frequency details efficiently. A view-based inpainting network refines textures ensuring smooth, continuous appearance. PHAF generalizes to novel viewpoints and leverages a parametric hand model for accurate articulations, making it compatible with standard graphics engines. Experiments show it is comparable to existing methods in visual fidelity while drastically reducing texture generation time by 30 times, enabling practical AR/VR applications.

</details>


### [57] [From 3D Perception to Safety Reasoning: A Graph-Based Framework for Real-Time Underground Mine Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03460)
*Pasindu Ranasinghe,Simit Raval,Dibyayan Patra,Bikram Banerjee,Ismet Canbulat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合3D语义感知、不确定性异常检测、规则检查、边缘LLM推理和GraphRAG记忆分析的连续监测框架，用于地下煤矿安全监控。该系统将彩色3D点云转化为结构化安全推理输出，通过场景图和时间图实现显式知识表示。在115个危险场景中，规则检查覆盖57%，结合LLM推理提升至76%，加入记忆推理后达93%。感知模型在30 FPS下实现92.7%准确率且内存占用低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下煤矿环境中人员与重型设备在共享、密闭、照明不良的空间中作业，存在设备接近违规、结构不稳定和遮挡盲区等难以预判的危险。传统监控系统（固定摄像头、规则接近警报）只能检测预定义事件，缺乏3D场景理解和上下文记忆能力，无法识别复杂或演变的危险。

Method: 提出连续监测框架，融合以下技术：1）彩色3D点云的语义感知；2）基于不确定性的异常检测；3）规则化危险检查；4）边缘设备上的大语言模型推理；5）基于GraphRAG的记忆分析。通过场景图和时间图作为显式知识结构，连接各推理阶段的感知输出。为克服标注数据稀缺，结合真实巷道扫描、受控物体放置和高保真长壁开采模拟生成多样化危险场景，并采用自监督预训练提升分割性能。

Result: 感知模型在30 FPS下达到92.7%准确率且内存占用低。在115个危险场景中，规则检查覆盖57%，结合上下文LLM推理提升至76%，加入基于历史记录的记忆推理后达93%。定性结果表明不确定性衍生的异常信号支持对超出预定义类别的分布外危险进行解释。

Conclusion: 基于图的知识表示结合3D感知与分层安全推理，为地下矿山监控中的智能决策支持提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Underground coal mining requires personnel and heavy equipment to operate within shared, confined, and poorly illuminated spaces where hazards such as equipment proximity violations, structural instabilities, and occluded blind spots are difficult to anticipate. Conventional monitoring systems, including fixed cameras and rule-based proximity alerts, can detect predefined events but lack the 3D scene understanding and contextual memory needed to identify complex or evolving hazards. This paper presents a continuous monitoring framework that converts colourised 3D point clouds into structured and traceable safety reasoning outputs. The framework combines 3D semantic perception, uncertainty-based anomaly detection, rule-based hazard checks, on-device LLM reasoning, and GraphRAG -based memory analysis to identify immediate hazards and interpret longer-term safety patterns. Scene and temporal graphs serve as the explicit knowledge structure, linking perception outputs across reasoning stages. To overcome the scarcity of labeled underground data, real roadway scans, controlled object placement, and high-fidelity longwall simulation were combined to generate diverse hazard scenarios, while self-supervised pretraining improved segmentation from limited annotations. The perception model achieved 92.7% accuracy at 30 FPS with low memory usage. Across 115 hazard scenarios, rule-based checks achieved 57% coverage, increasing to 76% with contextual LLM reasoning and 93% with memory-based reasoning using historical records. Qualitative results show uncertainty-derived anomaly signals support the interpretation of out-of-distribution hazards beyond predefined classes. Overall, graph-based knowledge representation combined with 3D perception and layered safety reasoning provides a practical foundation for intelligent decision support in underground mine monitoring.

</details>


### [58] [Mixed-Modality Dual Face-Hair Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03470)
*Quoc-Anh Bui-Huynh,Mai-Tuyen Lam,Dai-Anh-Tuan Nguyen,Thanh Duc Ngo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出混合模态双参考人脸-发型检索任务(DFHR)，构建了首个包含18万+注释三元组的基准DFHR-Bench，并提出了统一框架MFHC，通过解耦身份与发型嵌入、跨模态对齐和标记注入实现跨模态检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索任务未能处理混合模态下身份与发型两个语义独立属性的联合推理，缺乏支持跨模态面部分量检索的基准和方法。

Method: 构建包含双图像和图像-文本两种设置的DFHR-Bench基准（18万+三元组）；提出MFHC框架，通过token注入和多视角监督融合解耦的身份与发型嵌入。

Result: 建立了首个混合模态人脸-发型检索基准，提供了大规模标注数据集和统一检索框架。

Conclusion: DFHR和DFHR-Bench共同确立了跨模态身份感知、属性可控的视觉检索新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Dual Face-Hair Retrieval (DFHR), a new mixed-modality dual-reference task in image retrieval where a query consists of a face image specifying identity and a hairstyle reference expressed as either an image or text. Unlike prior retrieval settings, DFHR requires cross-component reasoning between two semantically independent attributes -- identity and hairstyle -- originating from heterogeneous modalities. This formulation demands localized feature disentanglement, cross-modal semantic alignment, and mixed-modality composition within a unified embedding space. We construct DFHR-Bench, the first benchmark for mixed-modality face-hair retrieval, comprising over 180K annotated triplets across dual-image and image-text settings, built via a multi-stage annotation protocol ensuring semantic and identity integrity. We further propose MFHC (Multimodal Face-Hair Combiner), a unified framework that fuses disentangled identity and hairstyle embeddings through token injection and multi-view supervision. DFHR and DFHR-Bench together establish a new paradigm for identity-aware, attribute-controllable visual retrieval across modalities.

</details>


### [59] [PersistGS: Differentiable Physics for Object Permanence in 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03479)
*Adrian Ramlal,John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PersistGS将可微刚体仿真与3D高斯溅射相结合，在物体被完全遮挡时通过物理规律驱动的SE(3)轨迹预测保持物体永久性，相比运动学外推在遮挡场景中PSNR提升+2.46dB，轨迹误差降低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3DGS方法在物体被完全遮挡时因光度监督消失而导致高斯表示退化，而基于生成先验的方法注重视觉合理性而非物理正确性，无法在处理碰撞、摩擦等接触事件时保持物体永久性。

Method: 将场景分解为逐物体高斯和碰撞网格，利用可微仿真从遮挡前轨迹估计摩擦和速度，通过SE(3)轨迹定位遮挡期间的高斯；引入质心轮廓损失从外观噪声中分离位置梯度。

Result: 在合成场景中，PersistGS比恒定速度外推PSNR高+2.46dB，与真实轨迹上界仅差0.19dB；轨迹误差比光度监督降低40%。

Conclusion: 将物理仿真与可微渲染耦合能有效恢复遮挡期间的物体永久性，物理约束的轨迹预测在视觉质量和物理正确性上均优于纯运动学或生成式方法。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods reconstruct time-varying scenes from synchronized multi-camera video using photometric supervision. When a moving object becomes fully occluded from all training cameras, this supervision vanishes: the Gaussians representing it receive no gradient signal and degrade. Existing approaches to incomplete observations in neural reconstruction rely on learned generative priors that prioritize visual plausibility over physical correctness.
  We propose $\textbf{PersistGS}$, a method that restores object permanence during occlusion by coupling differentiable rigid body simulation with 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our approach decomposes the scene into per-object Gaussians and collision meshes, estimates friction and velocity from the observed pre-occlusion trajectory via differentiable simulation, and uses the resulting SE(3) trajectory to position object Gaussians throughout the occlusion period. Because the predicted trajectory satisfies the governing equations of rigid body dynamics, it faithfully captures contact events (bounces, friction-based deceleration, direction changes) that kinematic extrapolation cannot model. We introduce a centroid silhouette loss that isolates positional gradients from appearance noise, yielding 40% lower trajectory error than photometric supervision. We evaluate using cameras withheld from training that observe the object during its occlusion. Experiments on synthetic scenes show that PersistGS outperforms constant velocity extrapolation by +2.46dB PSNR and comes within 0.19dB of a ground-truth trajectory upper bound.

</details>


### [60] [TrAction: Action Recognition with Sparse Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03490)
*Jan F. Meier,Felix B. Mueller,Alexander Ecker,Timo Lüddecke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏点轨迹的2.5D Transformer架构（TrAction），结合掩码轨迹预训练，实现了高效且避免外观/背景偏置的动作识别。该方法虽仅使用密集RGB输入的一小部分，但在多个基准上取得竞争性结果，并能与SOTA外观特征互补融合提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作识别模型依赖密集RGB视频体积，计算和内存开销大，且容易利用外观和背景捷径（如通过物体或场景而非特征运动来预测动作）。作者寻求一种高效且天然避免此类偏置的替代输入模态。

Method: 1) 使用稀疏点轨迹作为输入模态（2.5D表示）；2) 设计简单的Transformer架构处理轨迹特征；3) 提出掩码轨迹预训练策略，提升下游动作识别精度。

Result: Something-Something V2上达到45% top-1，EPIC-Kitchens-100上达到54% top-1；在时间反转敏感性指标上超越V-JEPA。与DINOv2融合提升8.7个点，与V-JEPA 2融合提升1.6个点。

Conclusion: 轨迹特征与基于外观的SOTA特征具有互补性，融合后可显著提升动作识别性能，证明了稀疏轨迹作为高效且无偏置输入模态的有效性。

Abstract: Modern action recognition models operate on memory- and compute-intensive dense RGB video volumes and frequently exploit appearance and background shortcuts, for example, predicting actions from objects or scenes instead of characteristic motion. We investigate an efficient alternative input modality that is largely free of such biases by construction: sparse point trajectories. To this end, we develop a simple transformer architecture for 2.5D trajectory-based recognition together with a masked-trajectory pretraining, which we show to substantially improve downstream action recognition accuracy. Despite using only a fraction of the dense RGB input, our method reaches 45% top-1 on Something-Something V2 and 54% on EPIC-Kitchens-100, and surpasses V-JEPA on time-reversal sensitivity. More importantly, we find trajectory features to be complementary to state-of-the-art appearance-based features. Fusing our pretrained model with DINOv2 and V-JEPA 2 improves top-1 accuracy on Something-Something V2 by 8.7 and 1.6 points, respectively. Code: https://github.com/ecker-lab/TrAction

</details>


### [61] [Low-Frequency Shortcuts in Texture-Driven Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03493)
*Utku Şirin,Cathy Hou,David Alvarez-Melis,Stratos Idreos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了纹理驱动领域中的捷径学习问题，发现这类领域主要依赖低频成分（LFCs）作为捷径，而非标准基准中的形状驱动因素。通过修剪低频成分，模型的分布内准确率提升最高达8%，对低频损坏的鲁棒性提升最高达40%，但会导致高频损坏的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有捷径学习研究均基于形状驱动的标准基准，但许多应用领域实际上是纹理驱动的，因此需要分析纹理驱动领域中的捷径学习现象及其特性。

Method: 通过分析纹理驱动数据集中的频谱行为，识别低频成分（LFCs）作为捷径；通过在训练集和测试集中修剪低频成分来消除捷径，并评估对分布内准确率、分布外鲁棒性的影响。

Result: 纹理驱动领域存在低频捷径，修剪LFCs后ID准确率提升8%，对低频损坏鲁棒性提升40%；OOD准确率在低频损坏下显著提升，但高频损坏下存在性能权衡。

Conclusion: 低频捷径是纹理驱动领域的主要问题，修剪低频成分可改善频谱平衡性，提升ID性能和低频鲁棒性，但高频鲁棒性存在权衡。

Abstract: Neural networks suffer from shortcut learning, where learned features generalize well to the training set but not to in-distribution (ID) or out-of-distribution (OOD) test sets. Existing studies are all based on a few standard benchmarks, which are shape-driven. Numerous application domains, however, are texture-driven. In this work, we present shortcut learning analysis for texture-driven domains, and compare it with that of a standard benchmark. We show that texture-driven domains suffer from low-frequency shortcuts. They make the majority of their decisions based on a few low-frequency components (LFCs) with a skewed spectral behavior, despite that their classification information is in higher-frequency, fine-grained details. Pruning LFCs from training and test sets eliminates the shortcut and provides a more balanced spectral behavior, improving the ID accuracy by up to 8%. We show that low-frequency shortcuts make the models highly vulnerable to OOD corruptions, leading up to 70% accuracy drop compared to the ID accuracy. Pruning LFCs significantly improves robustness to low-frequency corruptions, by up to 40%, and introduces a trade-off for high-frequency corruptions; the balanced spectral behavior provides a better generalization performance, whereas the increased dependence on high-frequency features reduces it. OOD accuracy depends on the interaction between these two factors.

</details>


### [62] [Characterizing Detectability in 3DGS Poisoning: A Stage-wise Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03499)
*Quoc-Anh Bui-Huynh,Thanh Duc Ngo,Xue Geng,Kaixin Xu,Wang Zhe,Xulei Yang,Ngai-Man Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建流程中多阶段中毒攻击的检测问题，提出了Poison-3DGS基准，系统分析了不同阶段（多视图图像、几何、训练动态、高斯参数）的可检测性差异，发现不同攻击在不同阶段表现出独特的取证信号，且后期阶段（如训练动态和高斯参数统计）提供了早期阶段无法观测到的强检测线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS中毒攻击研究主要关注攻击成功性，而防御和检测研究严重不足。3DGS重建流程的多阶段特性产生异构中间表征，取证信号具有阶段依赖性，亟需一个系统性的阶段式可检测性分析框架。

Method: 构建Poison-3DGS基准，涵盖多视角图像、几何、训练动态和高斯参数等多种阶段特定伪影，在多样化场景和攻击类型下进行系统性可检测性分析。

Result: 1）可检测性在不同阶段差异显著，没有一个阶段在所有攻击类型上始终占优；2）不同攻击具有独特的阶段特异性取证信号，检测有效性高度依赖于信号观测位置；3）后期阶段（训练动态、高斯参数统计）提供早期阶段无法观测的强检测线索。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个3DGS阶段依赖性可检测性的系统表征和标准化基准，为未来鲁棒可靠的3DGS系统研究奠定基础。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has rapidly emerged as a leading representation for real-time novel view synthesis, but recent work shows it is vulnerable to diverse poisoning attacks, including illusory object injection, computation cost amplification, and post hoc model watermarking. Despite this expanding threat surface, existing studies focus mainly on attack success, while defense and detection remain underexplored. From a detection perspective, a key challenge and opportunity arise from the multi-stage nature of the 3DGS reconstruction pipeline, which produces heterogeneous intermediate representations. Forensic signals for detecting poisoning are inherently stage dependent: an attack introduced at one stage may produce signals that emerge only at later stages. This motivates a stage-wise view of detectability that goes beyond single-stage evaluation. We introduce Poison-3DGS, a benchmark for stage-wise characterization of poisoning detection in 3DGS. It exposes stage-specific artifacts, including multi-view images, geometry, training dynamics, and Gaussian parameters, across a diverse set of scenes and attacks. Using it, we conduct a systematic study of detectability across pipeline stages. Our analysis reveals several insights. First, detectability varies significantly across stages, and no single stage consistently dominates across attack types. Second, different attacks exhibit distinct stage-specific forensic signals, so detection effectiveness depends critically on where signals are observed. Third, later-stage signals such as training dynamics and Gaussian parameter statistics provide strong cues not observable at earlier stages. Overall, our work provides a principled benchmark and the first systematic characterization of stage-dependent detectability in 3DGS, offering a foundation for future research on robust and reliable 3DGS systems.

</details>


### [63] [AvatarMix: Identity-Preserving Cross-Avatar Composition for Outfit Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03506)
*Zhaorong Wang,Yoshihiro Kanamori,Yuki Endo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AvatarMix提出了一种组合式3D虚拟形象换装范式，通过直接组合两个高保真度高斯虚拟形象的头部和身体来避免传统方法中的质量下降和交叉伪影问题，并采用SeamFix和FullbodyFix两级优化策略实现无缝连接与外观保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D虚拟形象换装方法面临两个主要挑战：将2D编辑提升到3D的方法会导致服装或身份质量下降，而分别建模身体和服装层的方法则容易出现交叉伪影。本文旨在克服这些问题，实现高保真的3D服装个性化。

Method: 提出AvatarMix组合范式，直接组合两个高斯虚拟形象的头部和身体；采用两级优化策略：SeamFix（局部扩散模块优化头发和颈部实现无缝连接）和FullbodyFix（全身优化恢复重定向后的服装外观）；基于网格的高斯表示适配鲁棒的网格重定向技术，精确重塑服装身体至用户体型。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在服装保真度和身份保留方面达到了最先进的水平，有效处理了不同身体形状的鲁棒重定向。

Conclusion: AvatarMix为3D服装个性化提供了新的视角，通过组合式范式规避了传统方法的核心问题，实现了高质量、无伪影的虚拟形象换装。

Abstract: Existing 3D avatar outfit transfer methods face distinct challenges: approaches that lift 2D edits to 3D often suffer from outfit or identity quality degradation, while those that separately model body and clothing layers are prone to intersection artifacts. We introduce AvatarMix, a compositional paradigm that bypasses these issues by directly composing the head and body from two high-fidelity Gaussian avatars. While this paradigm inherently preserves outfit quality and avoids intersections, it introduces challenges in creating a seamless join and maintaining appearance fidelity after body reshaping. To this end, we propose a two-tier refinement strategy: SeamFix, a localized diffusion module that refines hair and neck to ensure an artifact-free join, and an optional full-body refinement, FullbodyFix, that restores garment appearance when retargeting degrades the clothed body. Both operate on renders from an already 3D-consistent Gaussian avatar, which limits multi-view artifacts compared to 2D-to-3D lifting. To preserve the user's body identity, our mesh-based Gaussian representation enables the adaptation of a robust mesh retargeting technique, precisely reshaping the clothed body to the user's physique and robustly handling diverse body shapes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art results in outfit fidelity and identity preservation, providing a new perspective for realistic 3D outfit personalization. Project page: https://larsph.github.io/avatarmix/

</details>


### [64] [Structure-Guided Mixed Masked Pretraining and Spatial Continuity Regularization for Printed Circuit Board Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03508)
*Peitong Wang,Nuo Wang,Enxin Qin,Chengjin Yu,Hanyu Xuan,Yuanting Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段PCB缺陷检测框架，结合结构引导混合掩码预训练与空间连续性正则化，在DsPCBSD+数据集上达到85.5% mAP0.5和52.3% mAP0.5:0.95，优于多个强基线检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB缺陷检测中许多缺陷微小、低对比度且嵌入密集电路背景，现有方法难以有效检测。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1)预训练阶段采用稀疏卷积掩码预训练方案，利用结构引导混合掩码构建有意义的掩码输入，通过稀疏卷积重建管道抑制掩码区域无效响应，学习PCB结构先验；(2)微调阶段引入空间连续性正则化项，约束分散的正预测归属到同一缺陷实例，促进细长缺陷区域的紧凑定位。

Result: 在DsPCBSD+数据集上达到85.5% mAP0.5和52.3% mAP0.5:0.95，优于多个强基线检测器。

Conclusion: 提出的框架在工业AOI场景中实现了鲁棒的PCB缺陷检测，消融实验和定性结果进一步验证了有效性。

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) defect detection is an essential part of automated optical inspection (AOI); yet it remains challenging in practice because many defects are tiny, low-contrast, and embedded in dense circuit backgrounds. To address these issues, this paper presents a two-phase PCB defect detection framework that combines structure-guided mixed masked pretraining with spatial continuity regularization. In the pretraining stage, we design a sparse convolutional masked pretraining scheme to exploit unlabeled PCB images, where structure-guided mixed masking is used to construct informative masked inputs. The sparse convolutional reconstruction pipeline suppresses invalid responses from masked regions and enables the detector backbone to infer missing PCB structures from visible conductive patterns, thereby learning PCB structural priors. In the fine-tuning stage, the pretrained backbone is transferred to the downstream defect detection task. For the task, a spatial continuity regularization term is introduced during fine-tuning. This term constrains dispersed positive predictions assigned to the same defect instance and promotes more compact localization on elongated defect regions. Experiments on the DsPCBSD+ dataset show that the proposed method achieves 85.5% mAP0.5 and 52.3% mAP0.5:0.95, outperforming several strong baseline detectors. Ablation studies and qualitative results further confirm the effectiveness of the proposed framework for robust PCB defect detection in industrial AOI scenarios.

</details>


### [65] [EvoMemNav: Efficient Self-Evolving Fine-Grained Memory for Zero-Shot Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03509)
*Zuhao Ge,Xiaosong Jia,Chao Wu,Yuchen Zhou,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoMemNav提出了一种自演进、细粒度的视觉-语义记忆图框架，用于零样本具身导航，通过保留原始视图、分层组织、预算粗到细策略以及反射驱动的回写机制，在GOAT-Bench和HM3D上实现了SR/SPL的持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本具身导航方法中，检测器驱动的场景图将观测压缩为稀疏节点，丢失细粒度视觉证据并积累噪声；而基于3D重建的方法计算开销过大。因此需要一种高效且保留细粒度信息的记忆框架。

Method: (1) 构建VSMGraph（视觉-语义记忆图），将原始视图作为一等公民记忆，以轻量级语义线索和拓扑关系组织为房间-视图-对象层级结构；(2) 预算粗到细策略：粗阶段压缩搜索空间为候选区域，细阶段仅调用VLM进行针对性验证和决策；(3) 反射驱动回写：每个子任务后更新图附带的先验知识，编码累积环境信息以优化后续决策，无需重训练。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench和HM3D数据集上，覆盖目标物体、文本描述和图像目标三种模态，EvoMemNav在成功率（SR）和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）上均取得一致提升，展现出更好的多实例消歧能力、更少的提前停止以及更强的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: EvoMemNav通过保留细粒度视觉信息、层级记忆组织以及自演进的回写机制，有效解决了零样本具身导航中记忆构建的关键挑战，为长时域规划提供了一种高效且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Building memory is essential for long-horizon planning in zero-shot embodied navigation. Detector-centric scene graphs often compress observations into sparse nodes, discarding fine-grained visual evidence and accumulating noise, while 3D reconstruction-based methods remain computationally prohibitive. We present EvoMemNav, an efficient, self-evolving, fine-grained memory framework for zero-shot embodied navigation. EvoMemNav constructs a Visual-Semantic Memory Graph (VSMGraph) that keeps raw views as first-class memory and organizes them with lightweight semantic cues and topological relations into a room-view-object hierarchy, preserving fine-grained details for disambiguation and Stop verification. To scale to growing memory, we introduce a budgeted coarse-to-fine policy: a coarse stage compresses the search space into promising regions, and a fine stage invokes a VLM only for targeted verification and decision. Beyond static memories, EvoMemNav performs reflection-driven write-back after each subtask, updating graph-attached priors that encode accumulated environmental knowledge to refine future decisions without retraining. Experiments on GOAT-Bench and HM3D across object, text-description, and image-goal modalities show consistent gains in SR/SPL, with better multi-instance disambiguation, fewer premature stops, and stronger zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [66] [Knowledge-Preserved Model Tuning in Null-Space for Robust Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03539)
*Haoxuan Chen,Xianqin Liu,Jian-Fang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Null-Space Tuning (NST)框架，通过将高质量输入的特征分量限制在冻结权重的零空间内，低质量输入的恢复分量导向非零空间，在保持预训练知识的同时有效修复低质量视频，实现时空视频定位在混合质量场景下的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空视频定位方法主要关注高质量输入，忽视了真实场景中广泛存在的低质量视频问题；而LoRA等微调方法会不可避免地破坏预训练知识。

Method: 提出Null-Space Tuning框架，利用冻结权重零空间中添加向量不影响输出的几何特性，通过Quality-Adaptive Unit和Dual-Space Reparameterization合成可学习残差，将高质量输入分量限制在零空间，低质量输入恢复分量导向非零空间。

Result: 在Mixed-Quality基准上，NST优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: NST通过零空间调优策略，在保持预训练知识的前提下有效修复低质量输入，为混合质量场景下的视频定位提供了新思路。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding aims to localize object tubes based on textual queries. While recent methods have achieved remarkable success, they mainly focus on high-quality(HQ) inputs, neglecting the widespread presence of low-quality(LQ) videos in real-world scenarios. Although tuning methods like LoRA can adapt to degraded inputs, they inevitably disrupt pre-trained knowledge. To address this, we propose Null-Space Tuning (NST). This framework exploits the geometric property that adding vectors within the null-space of frozen weights to the layer input does not affect the output. Leveraging this, NST injects learnable residuals into input features that can be selectively invisible to the pre-trained backbone. Specifically, NST combines the Quality-Adaptive Unit and Dual-Space Reparameterization to synthesize these residuals by confining components for HQ inputs to the null-space, while directing restoration components for LQ inputs to the non-null space. As the frozen weights eliminate null-space components, we effectively rectify degraded inputs while preserving pre-trained knowledge for HQ inputs. Extensive experiments show that NST outperforms state-of-the-art methods on our Mixed-Quality benchmark.

</details>


### [67] [Attend to Anything: Foundation Model for Unified Human Attention Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03540)
*Wenzhuo Zhao,Ronghao Xian,Keren Fu,Qijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AAM（Attend to Anything Model），一个统一多模态注意力建模的基础模型，通过语言提示的层次化双曲嵌入和基于Fokker-Planck方程的流体动力学视角，统一了图像、视频和视听任务的注意力建模，在16个基准上平均超越SOTA 6%，视频推理速度提升约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力（显著性）建模方法在模态、场景和任务形式化上高度碎片化，模型容量和数据规模增加后仍局限于特定场景和任务，无法在实际应用中泛化。本文旨在解决这一根本性局限。

Method: 1) 将注意力重新形式化为一种认知蕴含关系，组织成从一般到特定的层次结构，通过双曲空间中的层次化语言提示嵌入实现。2) 采用流体动力学视角统一静态图像和动态视频注意力，将视频帧注意力建模为受Fokker-Planck方程支配的扩散时间演化过程。

Result: 在16个基准数据集上，AAM在各种场景下平均超越SOTA方法6%，视频推理速度提升约4倍。

Conclusion: AAM为注意力及显著性相关任务的未来研究提供了一个有原理基础的统一框架。

Abstract: Existing human attention (saliency) modeling methods persist as highly fragmented across modalities, scenes, and task formulations. Consequently, even with increasing model capacity and data scale, current models predominantly remain scene-dependent and task-specific, failing to practically generalize in real-world applications. To address the fundamental limitations, we present the Attend to Anything Model (AAM), a multi-modal foundation model that unifies attention modeling across various image, video, and audio-visual tasks and scenes. AAM reformulates attention as a cognitive entailment relationship organized in a general-to-specific hierarchy, implemented through language prompts with hierarchical embeddings in hyperbolic space. Furthermore, to unify static image and dynamic video attention, we adopt a fluid-dynamics perspective, formulating video-frame attention as a diffusive temporal evolution governed by the Fokker--Planck equation. Extensive experiments on 16 benchmarks demonstrate that AAM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods by an average of 6\% across various scenarios, while achieving approximately a 4$\times$ speedup in video inference. Overall, these results demonstrate that AAM provides a principled foundation for future research on attention and saliency-related tasks. The dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/wz-zhao/Attend-to-Anything.

</details>


### [68] [\textsc{CR-Seg}: Attention-Guided and CoT-Enhanced Coarse-to-Refined Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03564)
*Yifan Cao,Xiaocui Yang,Faxian Wan,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CR-Seg是一个两阶段粗细粒度推理分割框架，通过注意力图引导和全局到局部的思维链推理，解决跨模态对齐和推理-答案不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割方法面临两个主要局限：一是依赖学习语义桥接多模态大语言模型和分割模型，导致跨模态对齐困难；二是依赖显式空间提示（如边界框）会丢失整体响应语义。因此需要一种能同时解决对齐问题和保持语义完整性的方法。

Method: 提出CR-Seg两阶段框架：1) 设计EAP模块提取注意力图进行粗定位并选择信息点；2) 引入GLCoT（全局到局部思维链）引导模型从全局场景逐步推理到局部目标细节，缓解推理-答案不一致。粗定位结果和点提示输入SAM进行掩码细化。

Result: 在推理分割基准上的大量实验验证了CR-Seg的有效性。

Conclusion: CR-Seg通过粗到细的两阶段设计，结合注意力图引导和全局到局部的思维链推理，有效解决了推理分割中的跨模态对齐和推理-答案不一致问题。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation aims to segment target objects described by complex language through joint visual-textual reasoning. Existing methods typically rely on either learned semantic tokens to bridge Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and segmentation models, suffering from difficult cross-modal alignment, or explicit spatial prompts such as bounding boxes, which may lose holistic response semantics. To address these limitations, we propose Attention-Guided and CoT-Enhanced Coarse-to-Refined Reasoning Segmentation, termed CR-Seg, a two-stage framework for coarse-to-refined reasoning segmentation. Specifically, we design an Extract Attention Maps and Points (EAP) module to extract attention maps for coarse target localization and select informative points, both of which are fed into SAM for mask refinement. To alleviate reasoning--answer inconsistency, we further introduce Global-to-Local Chain-of-Thought (GLCoT), which guides the model to reason progressively from global scene context to local target details. Extensive experiments on reasoning segmentation benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of CR-Seg.

</details>


### [69] [Efficient Transformer-Based Localized Patch Sampling for Choroid Plexus Segmentation in Multiple Sclerosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03566)
*Po-Jui Lu,Alessandro Cagol,Mario Ocampo-Pineda,Federico Spagnolo,Marina Mastantuono,Andreea-Alexandra Aldea,Jannis Müller,Özgür Yaldizli,Matthias Weigel,Lester Melie-Garcia,Roberta Magliozzi,Maria Pia Sormani,Ludwig Kappos,Jens Kuhle,Cristina Granziera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SwinUNETR和定向脑室周小斑块采样的自动化脉络丛分割方法，在MS患者MRI数据上实现了高精度（DSC 0.868）且计算量降低99%（91.8 vs 22,080 GFLOPs），显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧脑室脉络丛（LVCP）已被证实是MS相关身体残疾和神经炎症的重要影像生物标志物，但其手动分割极为繁琐，限制了在大规模临床试验和纵向评估中的应用。因此需要开发一种自动化、高效且精准的分割方法。

Method: 采用SwinUNETR架构，以32×32×32体素的小斑块进行定向脑室内和脑室周采样训练，与3D UXNET模型进行对比。使用Dice相似系数（DSC）、95% Hausdorff距离（HD95）和计算量（GFLOPs）作为评估指标，基于两个MS主导队列的三组数据（n=177, n=177, 扩展测试集n=388）进行回顾性评估。

Result: 在扩展测试集上，SwinUNETR在MPRAGE+FLAIR组合下取得平均DSC 0.868（95% CI: 0.863-0.872），显著优于UXNET（DSC 0.858, p<0.0001）；单FLAIR输入时DSC达0.863，HD95为1.86 mm（UXNET为3.00 mm）；计算量降低99%（91.8 vs 22,080 GFLOPs）。

Conclusion: 通过集成局部斑块采样与SwinUNETR架构，该方法提供了准确、稳健且统计上显著优于现有领先模型的LVCP分割方案，其巨大的计算成本降低使其非常适合在临床和研究环境中广泛部署。

Abstract: Background: The lateral ventricle choroid plexus (LVCP) is gaining recognition as a key imaging biomarker for multiple sclerosis (MS) related to physical disability and neuroinflammation. Yet, manual segmentation of the LVCP is highly tedious, restricting its use in broad clinical trials and longitudinal assessments. This research aims to develop a SwinUNETR-driven pipeline that leverages targeted intra- and peri-ventricular small patch sampling to automatically segment the LVCP in MS from both standalone and multi-modal MRI inputs. Methods: We retrospectively assessed 3T MRI scans across three sets of data stemming from two separate MS-dominant cohorts (Dataset 1: n=177; Dataset 2: n=177; expanded test set: n=388). Our method employed a SwinUNETR architecture trained on 32x32x32 voxel patches, benchmarking it against the 3D UXNET model. The primary metric for evaluation was the Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC), supplemented by computational demand (GFLOPs) and the 95th percentile Hausdorff Distance (HD95). Results: On the extended test set, the SwinUNETR model secured a mean DSC of 0.868 (95% CI: 0.863-0.872) with MPRAGE and FLAIR combined, showing a statistically significant gain over UXNET (DSC: 0.858 [95% CI: 0.853-0.862], p<0.0001). When restricted to standalone FLAIR inputs, the transformer-based approach sustained a high DSC of 0.863, while the spatial localization of UXNET worsened considerably (HD95: 1.86 vs. 3.00 mm). Importantly, the proposed framework lowered computational load by 99% (91.8 vs. 22,080 GFLOPs). By integrating localized patch sampling with a SwinUNETR architecture, this methodology offers an accurate, robust, and statistically superior alternative to current leading models for LVCP segmentation. Its vast reduction in computational cost makes it ideal for widespread implementation in clinical and research environments.

</details>


### [70] [Learned Non-Maximum Suppression for 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03568)
*Timo Osterburg,Stefan Schütte,Torsten Bertram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于学习的过滤模块（D2D-Rescore和GossipNet3D）替代启发式非极大值抑制（NMS），用于LiDAR 3D目标检测的后处理阶段，通过检测框间的关系建模提升检测性能，尤其在小型和稀有类别上表现显著，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 3D目标检测中，后处理阶段需要使用NMS过滤密集重叠的候选框，但传统NMS依赖启发式规则，无法充分利用检测框间的语义关系，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出两种学习型过滤模块：1) D2D-Rescore，基于Transformer的检测框间注意力机制（D2D attention）对检测分数重评分；2) GossipNet3D，将2D GossipNet适配到3D，在鸟瞰图上进行局部消息传递。同时引入与nuScenes评估协议对齐的度量感知匹配策略，保证训练和验证一致性。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，两种方法相比CircleNMS在mAP、NDS和真阳性质量上均有提升，尤其对小型和稀有类别效果显著，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 学习型的检测级过滤可有效替代启发式NMS，在不修改基础检测网络的前提下提升3D检测器的可靠性，为后处理提供了一种原则性的替代方案。

Abstract: Post-processing is a critical stage in LiDAR-based 3D object detection, where dense and overlapping proposals must be filtered for compact and reliable perception. This work introduces two learned filtering modules that replace heuristic non-maximum suppression (NMS) by leveraging relations among detections. D2D-Rescore employs transformer-based detection-to-detection (D2D) attention, while GossipNet3D adapts the 2D GossipNet concept to 3D through localized message passing in bird's-eye view. A metric-aware matching strategy aligned with the nuScenes evaluation protocol ensures consistent training and validation behavior, improving overall detection performance. Both approaches improve mean average precision (mAP), nuScenes detection score (NDS), and true positive quality compared to CircleNMS, particularly for small and infrequent classes, while adding minimal computational overhead. These results demonstrate that learned, detection-level filtering can enhance 3D detector reliability without modifying the base network, offering a principled alternative to heuristic suppression. Code is available at https://github.com/rst-tu-dortmund/learned-3d-nms .

</details>


### [71] [When Attention Collapses: Stage-Aware Visual Token Pruning from Structure to Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03569)
*Jiahui Wang,Kai Zhang,Mai Han,Huanghe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STS，一种两阶段视觉token剪枝框架，通过解耦结构保留与语义筛选，解决基于注意力分数的单指标剪枝导致特征多样性下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法仅依赖注意力分数，导致高注意力token集中在语义相似区域，降低了特征多样性并丢失关键上下文信息。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段利用基于排斥的采样机制最大化空间和结构多样性；第二阶段利用指令感知的交叉注意力精确过滤与提示无关的token。

Result: STS在多种评估中有效缓解了注意力选择带来的冗余，提升了保留视觉token的结构多样性和细粒度任务对齐能力。

Conclusion: 解耦剪枝过程为结构多样性和语义相关性两个阶段，能够比单指标注意力剪枝更好地保留关键上下文信息并提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities but suffer from significant computational overhead during inference. While visual token pruning offers a promising solution, existing methods predominantly rely on initial attention scores. This single-metric paradigm presents a critical flaw: high attention scores inherently collapse onto semantically similar regions, thereby severely reducing feature diversity and discarding vital contextual details. To address this, we introduce Structure-to-Semantics (STS), a novel two-stage visual token pruning framework that explicitly decouples the pruning process. The first stage employs a repulsion-based sampling mechanism to maximize spatial and structural diversity. The second stage leverages instruction-aware cross-attention to precisely filter out prompt-irrelevant tokens. This two-stage synergy constitutes the core of STS, first ensuring geometric coverage and then refining the retained tokens according to semantic relevance. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that STS mitigates the redundancy caused by attention-based selection, improving both structural diversity and fine-grained task alignment of the preserved visual tokens.

</details>


### [72] [Eliciting Complex Spatial Reasoning in MLLMs through Wide-Baseline Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03577)
*Hao Zhong,Muzhi Zhu,Shenyan Zeng,Anzhou Li,Cong Chen,Hua Geng,Duochao Shi,Wentao Ye,Tao Lin,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReasonMatch-Bench，一个评估多模态大模型在宽基线匹配（WBM）中空间推理能力的基准，并发现现有模型在该任务上表现远逊于人类（F1: 37.2 vs 84.0）。为此，作者构建了自动数据生成管道，并提出动态对应强化学习（DCRL）方法，通过图像级视点递进和点级对应课程学习提升WBM训练效果，实验表明DCRL显著提升了模型在WBM及相关空间基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在物理环境中进行空间推理时，缺乏对宽基线匹配（涉及几何理解、视角变化、细粒度感知和遮挡推理）的系统化评估与训练框架。

Method: 1) 构建ReasonMatch-Bench基准，按视点位移和匹配粒度分层覆盖室内/室外/物体中心场景；2) 设计可扩展数据生成管道，从RGB-D视频和SfM重建中自动提取宽基线视图对；3) 提出动态对应强化学习（DCRL），包含图像级视点递进和点级对应课程学习，通过可验证奖励优化WBM训练。

Result: 在90样本困难子集上，人类F1为84.0，最佳基线仅37.2；DCRL显著提升ReasonMatch-Bench性能，并泛化至其他空间基准，同时保持或轻微提升通用视觉理解能力。

Conclusion: DCRL方法有效弥合了当前MLLM在宽基线匹配任务上与人类水平的差距，为提升物理环境中空间推理能力提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Wide-baseline matching (WBM) requires integrating geometric understanding, viewpoint changes, fine-grained perception, and occlusion reasoning, making it a challenging testbed for spatial reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) deployed in physical environments. However, current MLLMs lack systematic evaluation and training frameworks for these capabilities. We introduce ReasonMatch-Bench, a benchmark stratified by viewpoint displacement and matching granularity across indoor, outdoor, and object-centric scenarios, and show that current MLLMs still struggle with fine-grained wide-baseline correspondence: on a difficult 90-sample subset, human annotators achieve 84.0 F1, while the best existing baseline reaches 37.2. To bridge this gap, we build a scalable data-generation pipeline that automatically extracts wide-baseline view pairs from large-scale video-3D corpora, including RGB-D videos and SfM reconstructions, yielding diverse and verifiable supervision. We further propose Dynamic Correspondence Reinforcement Learning (DCRL), which combines Image-Level Viewpoint Progression and Point-Level Correspondence Curriculum to improve WBM training through verifiable rewards without explicit CoT supervision. Extensive experiments show that DCRL substantially improves ReasonMatch-Bench and transfers to related spatial benchmarks, while maintaining general visual understanding performance with modest gains on several benchmarks.

</details>


### [73] [Diffusing in the Right Space: A Systematic Study of Latent Diffusability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03578)
*Tianxiong Zhong,Xingye Tian,Xuebo Wang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验发现，潜变量扩散模型中，隐空间的重建质量并不等同于生成质量。作者训练了大量不同正则化策略、架构和配置的视觉分词器，并引入了一个新指标——速度不可约方差（VIV），用于衡量轨迹交叉引起的速度歧义。实验表明，VIV是生成质量最稳定的预测指标之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究提出多种影响隐空间扩散友好性的属性（如语义可分性、仿射等变性、分布均匀性等），但这些属性往往仅在有限的分词器上验证，缺乏系统性的比较和泛化性验证。作者希望找出哪些因素对生成质量最具预测性。

Method: 系统性地训练大量视觉分词器，涵盖不同的正则化策略、架构和隐空间配置，并使用多种下游扩散骨干网络进行评估。在此基础上，引入新指标Velocity Irreducible Variance (VIV)，用于量化由轨迹交叉引起的速度歧义。

Result: 识别出多个与生成质量稳定相关的隐空间属性，且这些属性在不同实验设置下表现出强泛化性。VIV被发现是生成质量最稳定的预测指标之一。

Conclusion: 隐空间的扩散友好性不仅取决于重建保真度，还受多种结构属性影响。VIV作为一个反映轨迹交叉程度的新指标，能有效预测生成质量，为设计更优的潜扩散模型分词器提供了指导。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models leverage visual tokenizers to compress images into latent spaces for efficient generative modeling. However, better reconstruction quality of a tokenizer does not necessarily translate into better generation quality, suggesting that latent representations should be evaluated not only by fidelity but also by their diffusability. Recent studies have proposed diverse explanations for diffusion-friendly latent spaces, including semantic separability, affine equivariance, distribution uniformity, spatial structure, spectral smoothness, and manifold continuity. Yet these properties are often validated on a limited set of tokenizers, leaving it unclear which factors are most predictive of downstream generation quality and whether such conclusions hold beyond the specific settings in which they are introduced. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of latent diffusability by training a large collection of tokenizers with diverse regularization strategies, architectures, and latent configurations, and evaluating them with multiple downstream diffusion backbones. Our analysis identifies several latent properties that consistently correlate with generation quality and exhibit strong generalization across experimental settings. Beyond existing metrics, we introduce Velocity Irreducible Variance (VIV), a measure of velocity ambiguity induced by trajectory crossings. Extensive experiments show that VIV is one of the most stable predictors of generation quality.

</details>


### [74] [UnsOcc: 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction in Unstructured Scene via Rendering Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03581)
*Ye Wu,Ruiqi Song,Baiyong Ding,Nanxin Zeng,Junjie Cheng,Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UnsOcc，一种面向非结构化场景的多模态3D语义占用预测框架，通过RenderFusion双向渲染融合模块和GSRefinement细节感知辅助监督方法，有效解决了场景稀疏导致跨模态融合困难以及长尾分布导致预测性能下降的问题，在露天矿数据集和nuScenes数据集上均取得了最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化场景（如露天矿）中不规则障碍物和稀疏场景布局使得传统3D目标检测等感知方法效果不佳，而直接应用3D语义占用预测面临两大挑战：场景稀疏性阻碍有效的跨模态融合，以及更严重的类别长尾分布进一步降低预测性能。

Method: 提出UnsOcc多模态3D语义占用预测框架，核心包含两个模块：(1) RenderFusion，基于渲染的融合模块，通过双向渲染监督增强跨模态特征对齐；(2) GSRefinement，基于高斯泼溅的细节感知辅助监督方法，将稀疏的3D占用预测投影为密集的2D语义分割图，实现对长尾类别的有效监督。

Result: 在自建的露天矿非结构化场景数据集和nuScenes公开数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文验证了所提方法在非结构化场景下3D语义占用预测的有效性，为解决场景稀疏和长尾分布问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unstructured scenes present unique challenges for autonomous driving, as irregular obstacles and sparse scene layouts undermine the effectiveness of traditional perception methods such as 3D object detection. 3D semantic occupancy prediction has emerged as a prominent focus due to its ability to provide dense spatial representations by assigning semantic labels to individual voxels in 3D space. However, directly applying 3D semantic occupancy prediction to unstructured scenes remains challenging because scene sparsity hinders effective cross-modal fusion and the more severe long-tail distribution in these scenarios further degrades prediction performance. To validate the effectiveness of our approach, we construct a dedicated dataset of unstructured scenes collected from open-pit mines. Based on this, we propose UnsOcc, a multi-modal 3D semantic occupancy prediction framework that improves robustness in unstructured environments. At its core, we introduce a rendering-based fusion module, RenderFusion, which enhances cross-modal feature alignment through bidirectional rendering supervision. Furthermore, we propose GSRefinement, a detail-aware auxiliary supervision method based on Gaussian Splatting that projects sparse 3D occupancy predictions into dense 2D semantic segmentation maps, enabling effective supervision for long-tail categories. Extensive experiments on both the open-pit mine dataset and the nuScenes dataset demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [75] [World Models Meet Language Models: On the Complementarity of Concrete and Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03603)
*Yucheng Zhou,Wei Tao,Yiwen Guo,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PF-OPSD框架，让世界模型生成视觉未来推演，MLLM进行抽象推理，通过特权未来自蒸馏方法学习何时调用、验证和整合视觉模拟，在VRQABench和OpenWorldQA两个基准上分别提升10.6%和10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型能生成视觉未来推演但结果随机、可能视觉合理但任务错误；MLLM可进行抽象推理却缺乏具体视觉模拟。需确定何时视觉模拟有用、推演是否可信、如何影响最终答案，因此提出可控具身推理问题。

Method: 提出Privileged-Future On-Policy Self-Distillation (PF-OPSD)方法。训练时以真实未来视频和正确答案作为教师端特权上下文，评估学生策略生成的具体推理轨迹；部署时不依赖真实未来。构建VRQABench（可控空间前瞻）和OpenWorldQA（开放域物理预测）两个人工验证基准。

Result: PF-OPSD在VRQABench上超越基线10.6%，在OpenWorldQA上超越10.9%，同时增强了对噪声或冲突推演的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过特权未来在线策略自蒸馏，可有效学习视觉模拟与抽象推理的协同调用与验证机制，提升具身推理的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: World models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide complementary capabilities for predicting future outcomes from static visual observations. World models can generate concrete visual rollouts of possible futures, while MLLMs can reason abstractly over questions, goals, and rules. However, generated rollouts are stochastic and may be visually plausible but task-incorrect, making it necessary to determine when visual simulation is useful, whether a rollout is credible, and how it should influence the final answer. We formulate this problem as controlled concrete reasoning, where a model learns to invoke, verify, and integrate visual future simulation alongside abstract reasoning. To study this setting, we construct two human-verified benchmarks, VRQABench for controllable spatial lookahead and OpenWorldQA for open-domain physical prediction, and propose Privileged-Future On-Policy Self-Distillation (PF-OPSD). During training, PF-OPSD uses ground-truth future videos and answers only as teacher-side privileged context to evaluate on-policy concrete-reasoning trajectories, while the deployable student never observes true futures at test time. Experimental results show that PF-OPSD outperforms baseline by 10.6% and 10.9% on VRQABench and OpenWorldQA, respectively, while increasing robustness to noisy or conflicting rollouts. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/yczhou001/PF-OPSD.

</details>


### [76] [SkelHCC: A Hyperbolic CLIP-Driven Cache Adaptation Framework for Skeleton-based One-Shot Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03610)
*Yanan Liu,Anqi Zhu,Jingmin Zhu,Jun Liu,Hossein Rahmani,Mohammed Bennamoun,Farid Boussaid,Dan Xu,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SkelHCC框架，利用双曲几何编码人体骨架的层次结构，结合CLIP跨模态对齐和免训练缓存机制，实现单样本骨架动作识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法基于欧氏嵌入和底层运动线索，难以建模骨架数据的树状层次结构，导致跨模态对齐和泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出SkelHCC框架，包含：(1) EH-HCLIP模块，将骨架序列和动作语言嵌入共享双曲空间，利用双曲几何的负曲率和指数体积增长自然编码关节-部位-身体层次；(2) LLM引导的多粒度投票缓存(LMV-Cache)，实现免训练上下文推理。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D 60、NTU RGB+D 120和PKU-MMD数据集上一致超越现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 双曲空间能更有效地建模骨架数据的层次结构，结合CLIP跨模态对齐和缓存机制，显著提升单样本动作识别性能。

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition aims to understand human behaviors from body joint sequences and is especially challenging in the one-shot setting, where only a single labeled exemplar is available for each novel action. A key challenge is learning representations that capture the hierarchical and compositional structure of human motion while aligning effectively with high-level action semantics under extreme data scarcity. Existing approaches, largely based on Euclidean embeddings and low-level motion cues, struggle to model the tree-like organization of skeleton data, limiting cross-modal alignment and generalization to unseen action categories. We propose SkelHCC, a unified skeleton hyperbolic CLIP-driven cache adaptation framework for one-shot skeleton-based action recognition. SkelHCC introduces an Explicitly Hierarchical Hyperbolic CLIP (EH-HCLIP) module that embeds skeleton sequences and action language into a shared hyperbolic space. By leveraging the negative curvature and exponential volume growth of hyperbolic geometry, EH-HCLIP naturally encodes the joint-part-body hierarchy of human anatomy and yields structurally consistent cross-modal representations. To support efficient one-shot adaptation, SkelHCC further integrates a training-free LLM-guided Multi-granularity Voting Cache (LMV-Cache) for context-aware inference. Experiments on NTU RGB+D 60, NTU RGB+D 120, and PKU-MMD demonstrate that SkelHCC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [77] [TurtleAI: Benchmarking Multimodal Models for Visual Programming in Turtle Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03626)
*Chao Wen,Jacqueline Staub,Adish Singla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TurtleAI基准测试（含823个任务），评估20+视觉语言模型在教育导向的视觉编程（Turtle Graphics）中的表现，发现多数模型成功率低于30%。通过少量种子样本合成数据微调Qwen2-VL-72B，在真实任务上提升约20%性能。失败分析表明，GPT-4o在空间推理和精确视觉复制上存在困难，而微调主要改善了视觉推理与代码实现的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在视觉编程方面的研究主要聚焦于生产力场景，而针对教育导向的视觉编程任务（如Turtle Graphics）的表现尚不明确，且限制模型在此类任务上性能的关键因素也未得到系统分析。

Method: 构建TurtleAI基准（823个基于真实Turtle Graphics任务的样本），评估20+个VLM；提出一种仅需少量种子样本的数据生成技术，用于微调Qwen2-VL-72B模型。

Result: 多数VLM在TurtleAI上的成功率低于30%；微调Qwen2-VL-72B后在真实任务上提升约20%性能。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在教育导向的视觉编程任务上表现不佳，主要受限于空间推理与精确视觉复制能力；通过合成数据微调可显著提升视觉推理与代码实现的对齐程度，从而改善性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have been explored for visual programming, where they generate code to solve visual tasks. However, most prior work focuses on visual programming for productivity; it remains unclear how well current VLMs perform on education-oriented visual programming and what factors limit their performance. To bridge this gap, we introduce TurtleAI, a benchmark containing 823 tasks curated based on real-world visual programming tasks in the Turtle Graphics domain. Solving these tasks requires models to perceive geometric patterns, reason about spatial relationships, and synthesize Python code that faithfully reproduces geometric patterns. We evaluate 20+ VLMs, including GPT-5, GPT-4o, and Qwen2-VL-72B, and find that they struggle significantly, with most achieving success rates below 30%. To address these limitations, we propose a data generation technique that requires only a small set of seed samples. Fine-tuning Qwen2-VL-72B on the resulting synthetic data yields an improvement of about 20% on real-world tasks. Our failure analysis reveals that GPT-4o struggles with spatial reasoning and precise visual replication, whereas fine-tuning primarily improves the alignment between visual reasoning and code implementation.

</details>


### [78] [VidMsg: A Benchmark for Implicit Message Inference in Short Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03635)
*Issar Tzachor,Michael Green,Rami Ben-Ari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VidMsg基准，用于评估短视频中隐含信息（消息）的理解能力。该基准包含400个YouTube片段、9个主题领域和52个细粒度目标消息，通过消息优先的管道构建。实验表明现有模型在此任务上表现不佳，因为需要语用推理和上下文线索整合。作者还提出了基线方法VidVec-Msg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准多关注显式视觉对象和动作识别，缺乏对短视频中隐含信息（创作者意图、潜台词）的系统性评估。作者希望填补这一空白，推动模型从表面内容理解迈向深层语义推理。

Method: 采用消息优先的管道：LLM将目标消息转化为间接搜索场景→检索候选片段→人工标注保留隐含传达消息的片段。构建双向消息-片段检索任务和多选QA诊断任务。

Result: 现代视频-语言和检索模型在VidMsg上表现不佳，因为任务需要语用推理、上下文线索整合以及语义相近消息的区分能力。VidVec-Msg基线方法提升了消息导向检索性能，但仍有较大改进空间。

Conclusion: 隐含消息理解是短视频理解的独特挑战，现有模型能力不足，VidMsg为这一方向提供了可靠的评估基准，未来工作可在此基础上进一步提升模型的消息理解能力。

Abstract: Understanding short online videos involves more than identifying visible objects and actions; video makers often include an underlying message or purpose in the clip. We introduce VidMsg, a benchmark for evaluating implicit message understanding in short, internet-native video clips. VidMsg contains 400 YouTube-derived clips across 9 practical topic areas and 52 fine-grained target messages, covering domains such as career and finance, education, health and well-being, culture, safety, sustainability, and lifestyle. VidMsg is constructed through a message-first pipeline: an LLM first translates target messages into indirect search scenarios, which are used to retrieve candidate clips. Human annotators then retain clips that convey the intended message without being overly explicit. VidMsg is designed primarily for bidirectional message-clip retrieval for scalable applications such as video search and recommendation, where systems must capture holistic video understanding. In addition to retrieval, VidMsg includes a diagnostic multiple-choice QA benchmark, where models select the intended message of a clip from semantically related alternatives. Experiments with contemporary video-language and retrieval models show that strong models often fail on VidMsg, because the task requires pragmatic inference, integration of contextual cues, and discrimination among semantically close messages. We also introduce VidVec-Msg, a baseline method that improves message-oriented retrieval while leaving substantial headroom for future work.

</details>


### [79] [A Benchmark for Semi-supervised Multi-modal Crowd Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03646)
*Haoliang Meng,Xiaopeng Hong,Yabin Wang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个半监督多模态人群计数基准，提出了标准化协议和多种代表性基线方法，并进行了系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督多模态人群计数任务尚未被探索，缺乏统一的基准和标准化评估协议。

Method: 首先制定半监督多模态设置与标准化协议（含不同标注比率的数据划分）；其次适配多类基线方法（全监督多模态方法+半监督单模态方法）；最后在基准上系统评估。

Result: 构建了首个半监督多模态人群计数基准，通过多种基线方法的评估建立了参考基准点。

Conclusion: 为半监督多模态人群计数研究提供了标准化基准和参考基线，代码与数据划分将开源。

Abstract: This paper constructs the first benchmark on semi-supervised multi-modal crowd counting. To lay the foundation for this unexplored task, we first formulate the semi-supervised multi-modal setting and a standardized protocol that specifies the labeled-unlabeled data partition across different labeled ratios. Next, to establish solid reference points, we carefully tailor a diverse set of representative baselines, including existing fully supervised multi-modal methods and semi-supervised single-modal methods. Then, we carefully evaluate their performance under our proposed benchmark. Codes and the data partition will be released on https://github.com/HenryCilence/Semi-supervised-Multimodal-Crowd-Counting.

</details>


### [80] [Graph Regularized Non-negative Reduced Biquaternion Matrix Factorization for Color Image Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03654)
*Hailang Wu,Yonghe Liu,Bingxuan Yu,Chaoqian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出图正则化非负简化双四元数矩阵分解（GNRBMF）模型，通过引入图拉普拉斯正则项保留数据局部几何结构，在彩色图像识别中取得优于或持平现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非负简化双四元数矩阵分解（NRBMF）仅关注重构精度，未利用图像数据的局部几何结构，限制了学习到的低维特征的判别能力。

Method: 提出GNRBMF模型，在简化双四元数系数矩阵中引入图拉普拉斯正则化，使原始空间中邻近样本在特征空间中保持相似表示；设计分量式交替投影梯度算法求解优化问题并分析收敛性。

Result: 实验表明，在多个测试设定下，GNRBMF取得了有竞争力或更优的识别性能。

Conclusion: GNRBMF通过图正则化有效提升了NRBMF的判别能力，在彩色图像识别任务中表现优越。

Abstract: Non-negative reduced biquaternion matrix factorization (NRBMF) uses the product of reduced biquaternion (RB) matrices to incorporate the non-negativity constraints of color image pixels into the factorization process. However, NRBMF mainly focuses on reconstruction accuracy and does not exploit the local geometric structure of image data, which may limit the discriminative ability of the learned low-dimensional features. To address this issue, we propose a graph regularized non-negative reduced biquaternion matrix factorization (GNRBMF) model for color image recognition. The proposed model incorporates a graph Laplacian regularizer into the reduced biquaternion coefficient matrix, encouraging nearby samples in the original space to have similar representations in the learned feature space. Meanwhile, GNRBMF retains the non-negativity-preserving property of NRBMF in the reduced biquaternion domain. To solve the optimization problem, a component-wise alternating projected gradient algorithm is derived, and its convergence properties are analyzed. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed GNRBMF model achieves competitive or superior recognition performance in some tested settings.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Single Solution: Multi-Hypothesis Collaborative Deep Unfolding Network for Image Compressive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03666)
*Wenxue Cui,Hualin Li,Yuhang Qin,Yifu Xu,Xiaopeng Fan,Debin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多假设协作深度展开CS网络(MHC-DUN)，通过联合优化多个解空间来显式建模和利用多个假设，在梯度下降中使用AlphaNet动态预测空间变化步长，在近端映射中设计多假设协作模块联合优化多个解，最终在CS重建任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度展开网络(CS-DUNs)主要将推理限制在单一解空间，忽略了CS问题固有的病态性（ill-posedness），该性质允许多个合理候选假设的存在。

Method: 基于近端梯度下降(PGD)算法，在多假设范式下联合执行梯度下降和近端映射：(i) 梯度下降：设计AlphaNet动态预测空间变化步长，实现多个解的协作梯度更新；(ii) 近端算子：设计多假设协作近端映射模块，利用假设内和假设间相关性先验联合优化多个解；设计复合损失函数平衡测量保真度、假设多样性和重建精度。

Result: 实验结果表明所提出的CS方法优于现有CS网络。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多个假设并设计相应的协作机制，可以有效解决CS问题的病态性，提升重建性能。

Abstract: Recent deep unfolding networks (DUNs) have advanced Compressive Sensing (CS) by effectively integrating iterative optimization with deep learning architectures. However, most CS approaches predominantly confine their inference to a single solution space, neglecting the inherent ill-posedness of CS problems that intrinsically permits multiple plausible candidate hypotheses. In this paper, a novel Multi-Hypothesis Collaborative Deep Unfolding CS Network (MHC-DUN) is proposed, which explicitly models and leverages multiple hypotheses by jointly optimizing across diverse solution spaces. Specifically, following the Proximal Gradient Descent algorithm, MHC-DUN jointly performs gradient descent and proximal mapping within this multi-hypothesis paradigm. i) For gradient descent, a well-designed AlphaNet is introduced to dynamically predict spatially varying step sizes for all hypotheses, enabling collaborative gradient updates across multiple solutions. ii) For proximal operator, a sophisticated multi-hypothesis collaborative proximal mapping module is designed, which leverages both intra-hypothesis and inter-hypothesis correlation priors to jointly refine multiple solutions. To enable end-to-end training, a novel composite loss function is designed, which balances measurement fidelity, hypothesis diversity, and reconstruction accuracy, encouraging exploration of complementary solutions while maintaining reconstruction fidelity. Experimental results reveal that the proposed CS method outperforms existing CS networks.

</details>


### [82] [A Fast Methane Detection Pipeline on Board Satellites Based on Mag1c-SAS and LinkNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03675)
*Jonáš Herec,Vít Růžička,Rado Pitoňák,Jan Sedmidubsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mag1c-SAS算法，相比现有最优方法加速约80倍，结合轻量级LinkNet模型有效降噪，在STARCOP和EMIT-MSeg数据集上显著提升甲烷检测性能，并通过硬件验证证实其适合星上资源受限环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱卫星图像可用于检测甲烷泄漏以减缓气候变化，但现有高光谱任务需人工指定目标区域，容易遗漏潜在事件；星上检测虽可克服下行链路速率限制，但传统甲烷检测方法计算复杂度高，难以在资源受限的星载硬件上运行。

Method: 本文测试了快速目标检测ACE和CEM方法（此前未用于甲烷检测），并提出Mag1c-SAS——当前最优Mag1c算法的加速变体；将其与基于U-Net和LinkNet的机器学习模型集成；引入两种新的波段选择策略；在STARCOP和新提出的EMIT-MSeg数据集上评估，并进行了硬件功耗/CPU/RAM分析。

Result: Mag1c-SAS比原始Mag1c快约80倍；结合LinkNet在EMIT-MSeg上AUPRC提升超30个百分点，在STARCOP上F1分数提升约4个百分点；硬件分析证实功耗和资源占用极低，适合星上部署。

Conclusion: 通过算法加速与轻量级模型结合，实现了高效、低功耗的星上甲烷检测方案，并开源了完整代码、模型和数据，为卫星遥感温室气体监测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Methane is a potent greenhouse gas, and detecting leaks early via hyperspectral satellite imagery can help climate change mitigation efforts. Meanwhile, many existing hyperspectral missions only capture areas manually targeted by operators, thus missing potential events of interest. To overcome slow downlink rates cost-effectively, onboard detection is a viable solution. However, traditional methane detection methods are too computationally demanding for resource-limited onboard hardware. This work accelerates methane detection by focusing on efficient, low-power algorithms. In particular, we test fast target detection ACE and CEM methods that have not been previously used for methane detection and propose Mag1c-SAS -- a significantly faster variant of the current state-of-the-art Mag1c algorithm. To explore their detection potential, we integrate them with a machine learning model based on U-Net and LinkNet. We evaluate our methods on the STARCOP dataset and a novel EMIT-MSeg dataset, which we introduce and open-source alongside a high-quality annotation strategy. The proposed Mag1c-SAS approach proves highly effective by operating ~80x faster than the original Mag1c approach, providing a visually similar, but noisier result. When additionally paired with the lightweight LinkNet approach, it effectively reduces noise, achieving AUPRC score improvements of over 30 pp on EMIT-MSeg compared to the baseline Mag1c approach, and an F1 score on STARCOP ~4 pp higher. We evaluate two novel band selection strategies and confirm the system's onboard viability through hardware profiling, demonstrating marginal power consumption and efficient CPU/RAM utilization. We release the final system in a user-friendly and lightweight PyPI library at: https://pypi.org/project/onboard-methane-detection/, alongside all experimental code, models, and data at: https://github.com/zaitra/methane-filters-benchmark.

</details>


### [83] [Investigating Adversarial Robustness of Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03713)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的对抗鲁棒性，发现大规模多模态对抗预训练是实现强鲁棒性迁移的关键因素，通过端到端多模态训练整合鲁棒视觉编码器可在强对抗攻击下显著提升性能；同时验证了鲁棒视觉表示是MLLM对抗训练的前提条件，并提出轻量级测试时随机变换作为非鲁棒MLLM的有效黑盒防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM通过视觉编码器（如CLIP）引入视觉输入，扩大了攻击面，易受视觉对抗扰动攻击。先前的防御方法通过保持与CLIP原始嵌入空间的对齐来兼容预训练MLLM，但这从根本上限制了鲁棒性的提升空间。因此，需要系统性地研究MLLM的对抗鲁棒性问题。

Method: 1) 提出诊断性CLIP对齐协议，可在完整MLLM训练前预测哪些鲁棒视觉编码器能有效迁移到多模态场景；2) 通过端到端多模态训练整合鲁棒视觉编码器；3) 对比受约束的即插即用基线方法；4) 验证鲁棒视觉表示作为对抗训练的前提条件；5) 引入轻量级测试时视觉随机变换作为非鲁棒MLLM的黑盒防御。

Result: 端到端多模态训练在强对抗攻击下，图像描述任务平均提升28 CIDEr分数，VQA准确率提升11.7%。从鲁棒骨干网络进行端到端对抗训练额外获得1.9 CIDEr分数和4.3% VQA准确率提升。测试时随机变换可将非鲁棒MLLM的对抗性能从接近零提升至与鲁棒模型相当的水平。鲁棒模型在白盒视觉越狱攻击下显著减少了有害内容的生成。

Conclusion: 大规模多模态对抗预训练（而非单模态规模）是强鲁棒性迁移的关键因素；鲁棒视觉表示是MLLM对抗训练的前提条件，直接对非鲁棒MLLM进行对抗训练会损害干净性能和对抗性能；测试时随机变换可作为有效的黑盒防御手段。

Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on vision-language tasks, but incorporating visual inputs through a vision encoder (e.g., CLIP) substantially expands the attack surface, making these models vulnerable to visual adversarial perturbations. Prior defenses typically preserve compatibility with pretrained MLLMs by enforcing strict alignment to CLIP's original embedding space during adversarial fine-tuning; while practical, this constraint fundamentally limits achievable robustness. We present a systematic investigation of adversarial robustness in MLLMs. We first introduce a diagnostic CLIP-alignment protocol that predicts, prior to full MLLM training, which robust vision encoders will transfer effectively to the multimodal setting, revealing that large-scale multimodal adversarial pretraining, rather than unimodal scale alone, is the critical factor for strong robustness transfer. Integrating such encoders into MLLMs via end-to-end multimodal training yields average gains of 28 CIDEr points on captioning and 11.7% VQA accuracy under strong adversarial attacks compared to constrained plug-and-play baselines. We further show that adversarial training applied directly to a standard non-robust MLLM degrades both clean and adversarial performance, establishing robust visual representations as a strict prerequisite, while end-to-end adversarial training from a robust backbone delivers additional gains of 1.9 CIDEr points and 4.3% VQA accuracy. Beyond training-time defenses, lightweight test-time visual stochastic transformations serve as an effective black-box defense for non-robust MLLMs, elevating adversarial performance from near-zero to levels comparable with robust models. Finally, we show that our robust models substantially reduce toxic generation under white-box visual jailbreak attacks. Code and pretrained weights will be released publicly.

</details>


### [84] [Text-to-Image Models Need Less from Text Encoders Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03715)
*Nurit Spingarn,Noa Cohen,Tamar Rott Shaham,Tomer Michaeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现文本到图像扩散Transformer模型实际上只依赖文本表征中两个简单方面：相邻token合并为词表示，以及通过位置嵌入编码的词序。作者构建了一种仅编码单个词义和词序、缺乏上下文信息的"位置标签词袋"表示，发现它足以生成与完整文本嵌入相当质量的图像，表明图像模型自身承担了解码复杂语言结构的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究文本到图像模型是否真正利用了文本嵌入中丰富的上下文信息（如组合性和属性绑定），以及文本表征的哪些方面对图像生成是必要的。

Method: 构建一种"位置标签词袋"（bag of position-tagged words）表征，仅编码单个词义和词序，去除所有上下文信息，并与完整文本嵌入进行对比实验。

Result: 该简化表征在视觉质量和文本保真度上与完整文本嵌入引导的生成效果相当，表明文本到图像模型通常不使用超越单个词义和词序的丰富上下文信息。

Conclusion: 与普遍认知相反，文本到图像扩散Transformer模型实际上并不需要文本嵌入中编码的复杂上下文信息，图像模型自身能够解码复杂的语言结构。

Abstract: Text-to-image models rely on text prompts as their primary interface to human intent. Prompts are encoded by a text encoder into embeddings that condition the image generation process. Beyond individual token meanings, text embeddings encode contextual information across the full prompt, such as compositionality and attribute binding. However, whether image models actually exploit this richer information remains underexplored. Here, we address the question: Which aspects of text representation are essential for image generation? We show that text-to-image diffusion transformer-based models commonly rely only on two relatively straightforward aspects of text representations: (i) the merging of adjacent tokens into a word representation, for words spanning multiple tokens, and (ii) word order, which is imprinted by the positional embedding of the text-encoder. To show this, we construct a new text embedding that encodes only individual word meanings and order but lacks any contextual information about the full prompt. We find that this bag of position-tagged words representation is sufficient to successfully guide image generation, achieving visual quality and text fidelity that are on par with full text embedding-guided generation. This demonstrates that, contrary to common belief, text-to-image models often do not use the rich information encoded in the text embedding beyond individual word meanings and word order. Instead, the decoding of complex linguistic structures is performed by the image model itself. Project webpage: https://nsping13.github.io/contextless-TTI/

</details>


### [85] [Beyond False Stability: High-Noise Drift Gating for Test-Time Adversarial Defenses in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03730)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现CLIP模型在弱噪声下对抗样本呈现异常的稳定性（虚假稳定），而在强噪声下对抗表征比干净样本更不稳定，并据此提出训练无关的漂移门控机制，只在检测到对抗性不稳定时才触发测试时防御，从而改善干净-鲁棒权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于随机扰动的测试时防御虽能提升鲁棒性，但常以损害干净准确率为代价，产生不利的干净-鲁棒权衡。本文旨在不牺牲干净精度的前提下提升对抗鲁棒性。

Method: 识别CLIP表征空间中弱噪声到强噪声的噪声区间转变现象：弱噪声下对抗样本呈现虚假稳定，强噪声下对抗表征比干净表征更不稳定。据此设计训练无关的漂移门控机制，以高噪声下的特征漂移作为轻量级门控信号，仅在检测到类似对抗的不稳定性时触发已有的测试时防御。

Result: 在13个数据集上一致改善干净-鲁棒权衡。8个细粒度数据集上，干净+对抗平均准确率：反攻击防御从65.7%提升至71.4%，噪声锚定从68.4%提升至73.2%；ImageNet及4个偏移变体上，从56.1%提升至66.2%和62.1%提升至67.6%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种训练无关、即插即用的漂移门控机制，利用CLIP在高噪声区间对抗表征与干净表征的稳定性差异来选择性触发防御，有效解决了测试时防御中干净-鲁棒权衡问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP show strong zero-shot generalization but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Adversarial training improves robustness but is computationally expensive, motivating test-time defenses. Recent approaches exploit how CLIP's visual representations respond to stochastic perturbations: aggregating predictions across noisy views, constructing Gaussian noise-averaged anchors and interpolating features toward them, or applying counter-perturbations. These strategies improve robustness but often degrade clean accuracy, yielding an unfavorable clean-robust trade-off. We revisit stochastic test-time defenses and identify an underexplored noise-regime transition in CLIP's representation space. Prior work explored perturbations mainly in the weak-noise regime, where adversarial examples can appear unusually stable (false stability). Our analysis shows this reverses as perturbation strength grows: beyond the weak-noise regime, adversarial representations become markedly more unstable than clean ones, giving a clearer separation signal. The transition is consistent across uniform and Gaussian noise, photometric and geometric transforms, datasets, and diverse attacks. It largely disappears in adversarially trained models, suggesting it is tied to the fragile local-basin geometry of adversarial representations in non-robust CLIP. We propose a training-free, plug-in drift-gated mechanism that uses high-noise feature drift as a lightweight gating signal to trigger existing test-time defenses only when adversarial-like instability is detected. Across 13 datasets it consistently improves the clean-robust trade-off. On eight fine-grained datasets, mean clean+adversarial accuracy rises from 65.7% to 71.4% for counterattack defenses and 68.4% to 73.2% for noise-anchoring; on ImageNet and four shifted variants, from 56.1% to 66.2% and 62.1% to 67.6%.

</details>


### [86] [Qwen-Image-Flash: Beyond Objective Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03746)
*Tianhe Wu,Kun Yan,Zikai Zhou,Lihan Jiang,Jiahao Li,Jie Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Ningyuan Tang,Shengming Yin,Xiaoyue Chen,Xiao Xu,Yilei Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yan Shu,Yixian Xu,Yanran Zhang,Zihao Liu,Zhendong Wang,Zekai Zhang,Deqing Li,Liang Peng,Yi Wang,Jingren Zhou,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从训练流程（数据组成、教师引导、任务混合）而非蒸馏目标的角度，系统研究了少步蒸馏的配方，提出了Qwen-Image-Flash模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步蒸馏工作多聚焦于蒸馏目标本身，忽视了训练流程（recipe）对学生性能的关键影响，本文旨在从这一互补视角进行系统探究。

Method: 以Qwen-Image-2.0为代表性模型，系统研究统一文本到图像生成与指令引导图像编辑蒸馏中的三个因素：数据组成、教师引导、任务混合。

Result: 揭示了若干非显而易见的训练行为，并基于分析结果开发了Qwen-Image-Flash模型。

Conclusion: 有效的少步蒸馏不仅需要精心设计的蒸馏目标，还需要对更广泛的训练流程进行有原则的组织。

Abstract: Few-step distillation has become an effective strategy for accelerating advanced visual generative models, yet prior work has largely focused on distillation objectives.
